- product: R-D; SMILES候选=未确认

备注：Figure 7e and page-7 prose propose reduction to Co(I) A, oxidative addition to Co(III) B, D2O coordination to C, four-centered C-D formation, product release, and Mn reduction back to A. A-D are proposed intermediates, not isolated preparations.
